+displayResult(boolean, Restaurant): void\n}\n\nclass GameController <<control>> {\n  -restaurant: Restaurant\n  -currentTurn: int\n  -maxTurns: int\n  -cli: RestaurantCLI\n  +startGame(): void\n  -executeTurn(): void\n  -processScheduledArrival(): void\n  -coordinateStaffActions(): void\n  -coordinatePaymentProcessing(): void\n  -processEndOfTurnUpdates(): void\n  -evaluateGameResult(): boolean\n}\n\n' ---------- restaurant and menu ----------\nclass Restaurant {\n  -tables: List<Table>\n  -customers: List<Customer>\n  -waitingQueue: Queue<Customer>\n  -waiter: Waiter\n  -chef: Chef\n  -cashier: Cashier\n  -revenueCents: int\n  -menuItems: List<MenuItem>\n  +registerArrival(): void\n  +findAvailableTable(): Table\n  +findOldestEligiblePayer(turn: int): Customer\n  +getWaiter(): Waiter\n  +getChef(): Chef\n  +getCashier(): Cashier\n  +getMenuItems(): List<MenuItem>\n  +recordPayment(): void\n  +releaseTableOf(customer: Customer): void\n  +getPaidCustomerCount(): int\n  +getAverageSatisfaction(): double\n  +getRevenueCents(): int\n  +handleAbandonment(Customer): void\n  +getUnhappyDepartureCount(): int\n}\n\nclass MenuItem {\n  -name: String\n  -priceCents: int\n  -preparationUnits: int\n  +getName(): String\n  +getPriceCents(): int\n  +getPreparationUnits(): int\n}\n\n' ---------- staff and table ----------\nclass Waiter {\n  +seatCustomer(Customer, Table): void\n  +takeOrder(Customer, MenuItem): void\n  +serveOrder(Customer): void\n}\n\nclass Chef {\n  +prepare(Order): void\n}\n\nclass Cashier {\n  +processPayment(Customer, Restaurant): void\n}\n\nclass Table {\n  -tableId: int\n  -occupant: Customer\n  +isAvailable(): boolean\n  +seat(Customer): void\n  +release(): void\n  +getOccupied(): Customer\n}\n\n' ---------- customer and order ----------\nclass Customer {\n  -customerId: int\n  -satisfaction: int\n  -status: CustomerStatus\n  -order: Order\n  -servedTurn: int\n  -readyTurn: int\n  +getId(): int\n  +getStatus(): CustomerStatus\n  +getSatisfaction(): int\n  +chooseMenuItem(): MenuItem\n  +assignOrder(Order): void\n  +getOrder(): Order\n  +markSeated(): void\n  +markServed(turn: int): void\n  +canFinishEating(turn: int): boolean\n  +markReadyToPay(turn: int): void\n  +canPay(turn: int): boolean\n  +markPaid(): void\n  +reduceSatisfaction(): void\n  +shouldAbandon(): boolean\n  +markAbandoned(): void\n}\n\nclass Order {\n  -orderId: int\n  -item: MenuItem\n  -completedUnits: int\n  -status: OrderStatus\n  +advancePreparation(): void\n  +isReady(): boolean\n  +markServed(): void\n  +markPaid(): void\n  +cancel(): void\n  +getStatus(): OrderStatus\n  +getPriceCents(): int\n}\n\nenum CustomerStatus #EAF1F7 {\n  WAITING\n  SEATED\n  ORDERED\n  SERVED\n  READY_TO_PAY\n  PAID\n  ABANDONED\n}\n\nenum OrderStatus #EAF1F7 {\n  PLACED\n  PREPARING\n  READY\n  SERVED\n  PAID\n  CANCELLED\n}\n\n' ---------- layout skeleton (hidden links keep the rows tidy) ----------\nRestaurantCLI -[hidden]right- GameController\nTable -[hidden]right- Waiter\nWaiter -[hidden]right- Chef\nChef -[hidden]right- Cashier\nCashier -[hidden]right- MenuItem\nCustomer -[hidden]right- Order\nOrder -[hidden]right- CustomerStatus\nCustomerStatus -[hidden]right- OrderStatus\n\n' ---------- control and boundary ----------\nRestaurantCLI \"cli 1\" <-right-> \"controller 1\" GameController\nGameController \"1\" -down-> \"restaurant 1\" Restaurant\nRestaurantCLI .down.> Restaurant : displays\n\n' ---------- composition (Restaurant owns these) ----------\nRestaurant \"1\" *-down- \"2\" Table : tables\nRestaurant \"1\" *-down- \"1\" Waiter : waiter\nRestaurant \"1\" *-down- \"1\" Chef : chef\nRestaurant \"1\" *-down- \"1\" Cashier : cashier\n\n' ---------- aggregation (customers arrive and leave; menu items are shared) ----------\nRestaurant \"1\" o-down- \"customers 0..*\" Customer\nRestaurant \"1\" o-down- \"0..*\" Customer : waitingQueue\\n{ordered, FIFO}\nRestaurant \"1\" o-down- \"3\" MenuItem : menuItems\n\n' ---------- domain associations ----------\nTable \"1\" -down- \"0..1\" Customer : occupant\nCustomer \"1\" -right-> \"order 0..1\" Order\nOrder \"0..*\" -right-> \"item 1\" MenuItem\nCustomer -down-> CustomerStatus : status\nOrder -down-> OrderStatus : status\n\n' ---------- dependencies ----------\nWaiter .left.> Table : seats\nWaiter .down.> Customer : seats / serves\nWaiter .down.> Order : creates\nWaiter .right.> MenuItem : uses\nChef .down.> Order : advances\nCashier .down.> Customer : charges\nCashier .up.> Restaurant : recordPayment,\\nreleaseTableOf\n\n' ---------- annotations ----------\nnote top of Restaurant\n  <b>COMPOSITION</b>\n  Restaurant creates and owns its Tables and staff;\n  they exist only as part of one Restaurant.\n  Customers are aggregated: they arrive and leave.\n  The waitingQueue and customers links both point\n  at Customer. Stage 1 has only Regular customers.\nend note\n\nnote bottom of Order\n  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>\n  status is private and changes only through\n  advancePreparation(), markServed(), markPaid(),\n  cancel(); illegal transitions are rejected.\n  Legal: PLACED → PREPARING → READY → SERVED → PAID\n  (PLACED → READY directly for a 1-unit dish);\n  PLACED / PREPARING / READY → CANCELLED if the\n  customer leaves. The price is read from the item.\nend note\n\nnote bottom of CustomerStatus\n  <b>CUSTOMER LIFECYCLE</b>\n  WAITING → SEATED → ORDERED → SERVED →\n  READY_TO_PAY → PAID; WAITING / SEATED / ORDERED\n  → ABANDONED when satisfaction reaches 0.\n  Served, eating and READY_TO_PAY customers\n  lose no satisfaction.\nend note\n\nnote left of Customer\n  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>\n  satisfaction and status are private and change only\n  through reduceSatisfaction() and the mark...() methods.\n  <b>TURN GUARDS</b>\n  canFinishEating(turn): one full turn after serving.\n  canPay(turn): only after the READY_TO_PAY turn.\n  Regular customers lose 8 per waiting turn.\nend note\n\nnote right of Waiter\n  <b>STAFF ROLES</b>\n  One class per role: the Waiter seats, takes orders\n  and serves; the Chef prepares; the Cashier takes\n  payment. Each does at most one task per turn.\nend note\n@enduml\n"
}

recordPayment,\\nreleaseTableOf\nCustomer .up.> Menu : chooses from\n\n' ---------- annotations ----------\nnote top of Restaurant\n  <b>COMPOSITION</b>\n  Restaurant creates and owns its Tables, Menu and\n  staff; they exist only as part of one Restaurant.\n  Customers are aggregated: they arrive and leave.\n  The waitingQueue and customers links both point\n  at Customer.\nend note\n\nnote bottom of Order\n  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>\n  status is private and changes only through\n  advancePreparation(), markServed(), markPaid(),\n  cancel(); illegal transitions are rejected.\n  Legal: PLACED → PREPARING → READY → SERVED → PAID\n  (PLACED → READY directly for a 1-unit dish);\n  PLACED / PREPARING / READY → CANCELLED if the\n  customer leaves.\n  billCents is set once in the constructor with no\n  setter: the price is locked when ordered.\nend note\n\nnote bottom of CustomerStatus\n  <b>CUSTOMER LIFECYCLE</b>\n  WAITING → SEATED → ORDERED → SERVED →\n  READY_TO_PAY → PAID; WAITING / SEATED / ORDERED\n  → ABANDONED when satisfaction reaches 0.\n  Served, eating and READY_TO_PAY customers\n  lose no satisfaction.\nend note\n\nnote left of Customer\n  <b>POLYMORPHISM</b>\n  getSatisfactionLoss(), calculateBillCents(),\n  chooseMenuItem() and onServed() are overridden.\n  GameController and Waiter call them on Customer\n  and never check the type.\n  Loss per waiting turn: Regular 8, VIP 5, Critic 12.\n  <b>FOOD CHOICE</b>\n  Regular and VIP: menu.getItem(customerId % menu.size()).\n  Critic overrides it (see CRITIC RULE).\n  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>\n  satisfaction is private; subclasses change it only\n  through adjustSatisfaction() (clamped 0..100).\n  <b>TURN GUARDS</b>\n  Eats only if currentTurn > servedTurn;\n  pays only if currentTurn > readyTurn.\nend note\n\nnote right of Waiter\n  <b>BILLING</b>\n  takeOrder(): bill = customer.calculateBillCents(price),\n  stored in the new Order (VIP pays 90%).\nend note\n\nnote right of CriticCustomer\n  <b>CRITIC RULE</b>\n  Loses 12 per waiting turn. Always orders the most\n  expensive menu item (Pasta, $18.00). Served more\n  than 2 turns after ordering: loses an extra 10\n  (onServed).\nend note\n\nnote left of RegularCustomer #E8E8E8\n  <b>KEY</b>\n  <back:#CCEBD2>  green  </back> new in Stage 2\n  <back:#FAE8A6>  yellow  </back> changed since Stage 1\n  <back:#DCEBF7>  blue  </back> unchanged\n  <b>Changed since Stage 1</b>\n  Customer: abstract; chooseMenuItem() to chooseMenuItem(Menu);\n    + loss, billing and onServed hooks; + getServedTurn(),\n    getReadyTurn(); canFinishEating(turn) and canPay(turn)\n    removed (the turn guards are checked in GameController)\n  Order: getPriceCents() to getBillCents(); + billCents, turnTaken\n  Waiter: takeOrder(Customer, MenuItem) to\n    takeOrder(Customer, Menu, turn): Order; serveOrder + turn\n  Restaurant: registerArrival() to registerArrival(Customer);\n    recordPayment() to recordPayment(amountCents);\n    menuItems / getMenuItems() replaced by menu / getMenu();\n    findOldestEligiblePayer(turn) removed\n  GameController: + schedule\nend note\n@enduml\n"

+calculateBillCents(basePriceCents: int): int\n}\n\nclass CriticCustomer {\n  {static} -LATE_SERVICE_TURNS: int = 2\n  {static} -LATE_PENALTY: int = 10\n  +getTypeLabel(): String\n PAID\n  ABANDONED\n}\n\nenum OrderStatus #EAF1F7 {\n  PLACED\n  PREPARING\n  READY\n  SERVED\n  PAID\n  CANCELLED\n}\n\n' ---------- layout skeleton (hidden links keep the rows tidy) ----------\n' *-down- \"1\" Chef : chef\nRestaurant \"1\" *-down- \"1\" Cashier : cashier\nRestaurant \"1\" *-down- \"1\" Menu : menu\n\n' ---------- aggregation (customers arrive and leave) ----------\nRestaurant \"1\" o-down- \"0..*\" Customer : customers\nRestaurant \"1\" o-down- \"0..*\" Customer : waitingQueue\\n{ordered, FIFO}\n\n' ---------- menu ----------\nMenu \"1\" o-down- \"3..*\" MenuItem : items\nMenuItem <|-down- ComboMeal\nComboMeal \"0..*\" o-up- \"2\" MenuItem : components\n\n' ---------- domain associations ----------\nCustomer \"0..1\" -up- \"1\" Table : occupant\nCustomer \"1\" -right-> \"0..1\" Order\nOrder \"0..*\" -right-> \"1\" MenuItem\nCustomer -down-> CustomerStatus : status\nOrder -down-> OrderStatus : status\nCustomer <|-down- RegularCustomer\nCustomer <|-down- VIPCustomer\nCustomer <|-down- CriticCustomer\n\n' ---------- dependencies ----------\nGameController .[#777777].> Waiter\nGameController .[#777777].> Host\nGameController .[#777777].> Chef\nGameController .[#777777].> Cashier\nGameController .[#777777].> HappyHour\nGameController .[#777777].> Customer\nHost .right.> Table\nWaiter .left.> Table\nHost .down.> Customer : seats\nWaiter .down.> Customer : seats / serves\nWaiter .down.> Order : <<create>>\nWaiter .down.> Menu : reads\nWaiter .up.> HappyHour : applyDiscount\nChef .down.> Order : advances\nCashier .down.> Customer : charges\nCashier .down.> Order : reads bill\nCashier .up.> Restaurant : recordPayment,\\nreleaseTableOf\nCustomer .up.> Menu : chooses from\n\n' ---------- annotations ----------\nnote top of Restaurant\n  <b>COMPOSITION</b>\n  Restaurant creates and owns its Tables, Menu,\n  HappyHour and staff; they exist only as part of\n  one Restaurant. Customers are aggregated:\n  they arrive and leave. The waitingQueue and\n  customers links both point at Customer.\nend note\n\nnote bottom of Order\n  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>\n  status is private and changes only through\n  advancePreparation(), markServed(), markPaid(),\n  cancel(); illegal transitions are rejected.\n  Legal: PLACED → PREPARING → READY → SERVED → PAID\n  (PLACED → READY directly for a 1-unit dish);\n  PLACED / PREPARING / READY → CANCELLED if the\n  customer leaves.\n  billCents is set once in the constructor with no\n  setter: the price is locked when ordered.\nend note\n\nnote bottom of CustomerStatus\n  <b>CUSTOMER LIFECYCLE</b>\n  WAITING → SEATED → ORDERED → SERVED →\n  READY_TO_PAY → PAID; WAITING / SEATED / ORDERED\n  → ABANDONED when satisfaction reaches 0.\n  Served, eating and READY_TO_PAY customers\n  lose no satisfaction.\nend note\n\nnote left of Customer\n  <b>POLYMORPHISM</b>\n  getSatisfactionLoss(), calculateBillCents(),\n  chooseMenuItem() and onServed() are overridden.\n  GameController and Waiter call them on Customer\n  and never check the type.\n  Loss per waiting turn: Regular 8, VIP 5, Critic 12.\n  <b>ENCAPSULATION</b>\n  satisfaction is private; subclasses change it only\n  through adjustSatisfaction() (clamped 0..100).\n  <b>TURN GUARDS</b>\n  Eats only if currentTurn > servedTurn;\n  pays only if currentTurn > readyTurn.\nend note\n\nnote right of Waiter\n  <b>BILLING</b>\n  takeOrder(): bill = happyHour.applyDiscount(\n    customer.calculateBillCents(price))\n  VIP discount first, then Happy Hour; locked in\n  the new Order. VIP Burger: 1500, 1350, 1080 cents.\nend note\n\nnote right of HappyHour\n  <b>HAPPY HOUR</b>\n  Once per game, at turn start; active for that turn\n  and the next. 20% off orders taken; +15 satisfaction\n  (max 100) for customers waiting for a table or food.\nend note\n\nnote right of CriticCustomer\n  <b>CRITIC RULE</b>\n  Loses 12 per waiting turn. Always orders the most\n  expensive menu item (Salad + Pasta, $24.30, 3 units).\n  Served more than 2 turns after ordering: loses an\n  extra 10 (onServed).\nend note\n\nnote bottom of ComboMeal\n  <b>COMBO</b>\n  Stage 3 menu = Salad, Burger, Pasta + 2 ComboMeals\n  (5 entries), registered once at game setup.\n  Customer.chooseMenuItem(menu) picks from it.\n  price = (a.price + b.price) x 90%, rounded:\n  (sumCents x 90 + 50) / 100; units = a.units + b.units.\n  Salad + Burger $21.60, 2 units; Salad + Pasta $24.30,\n  3 units. Choice rule: customerId % menu.size(), so\n  indexes 3 and 4 are the combos.\nend note\n\nlegend left\n  <b>KEY</b>\n  <back:#CCEBD2>  green  </back> new in Stage 3\n  <back:#FAE8A6>  yellow  </back> changed since Stage 2\n  <back:#DCEBF7>  blue  </back> unchanged\n  <b>Changed since Stage 2</b> (nothing removed)\n  Customer: + recoverSatisfaction(), isAwaitingService()\n  Waiter: takeOrder(...) also takes HappyHour\n  MenuItem: public constructor (ComboMeal calls super)\n  Restaurant: + host, happyHour and their getters\n  GameController, RestaurantCLI: Host and Happy Hour steps\nend legend\n@enduml\n"

game -> hh : amount = getRecoveryAmount()\n    loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()\n      game -> vip : recoverSatisfaction(amount)\n      note right of vip : 100 stays 100 (capped)\n    end\n  end\n\n  game -> game : **2-3 Manager choices and staff actions** coordinateStaffActions()\n  note over game, cli : Steps 3-4 have nothing to do until a dish is READY\\nor a customer is served (drawn from turn 3)\n  game -> cli : choice = readWaiterChoice()\n  cli -> M : Waiter: SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT?\n  M --> cli : SEAT\n  opt choice == SEAT\n    game -> rest : t1 = findAvailableTable()\n    game -> waiter : seatCustomer(vip, t1)\n    waiter -> t1 : seat(vip)\n    waiter -> vip : markSeated()\n    note right of vip : WAITING to SEATED\n    note over rest : vip leaves waitingQueue once seated\n  end\n  note over game, cli : Chef: manager picks WAIT (no order exists yet)\n\n  game -> game : **5 Waiting and abandonment** processEndOfTurnUpdates()\n  game -> rest : customers = getCustomers()\n  loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()\n    game -> vip : reduceSatisfaction()\n    vip -> vip : adjustSatisfaction(-getSatisfactionLoss())\n    note right of vip : polymorphism: loss per waiting turn\\nRegular 8, VIP 5, Critic 12\\n100 to 95\n    game -> vip : leave = shouldAbandon()\n    opt leave (satisfaction == 0)\n      game -> rest : handleAbandonment(vip)\n      note right of rest : cancel order, free table, ABANDONED\n    end\n  end\n  game -> hh : endTurn()\n  note right of hh : remainingTurns 2 to 1\n\n  == Turn 2: Happy Hour recovery, Waiter takes the order (price locked), Chef finishes it ==\n\n  game -> game : applyHappyHourRecovery()\n  game -> hh : active = isActive()\n  loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()\n    game -> vip : recoverSatisfaction(15)\n    note right of vip : 95 to 100 (capped at 100)\n  end\n\n  game -> game : **2-3 Manager choices and staff actions** coordinateStaffActions()\n  game -> cli : choice = readWaiterChoice()\n  cli -> M : Waiter: SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT?\n  M --> cli : TAKE_ORDER\n  opt choice == TAKE_ORDER\n    game -> waiter : order = takeOrder(vip, menu, hh, 2)\n    waiter -> vip : item = chooseMenuItem(menu)\n    note right of vip : documented rule: Burger\\n(1500 cents, 1 unit)\n    waiter -> vip : bill = calculateBillCents(1500)\n    note right of vip : polymorphism: VIPCustomer\\noverrides it, 1500 x 0.9 = 1350\n    waiter -> hh : bill = applyDiscount(1350)\n    note right of hh : Happy Hour active: 1350 x 0.8 = 1080\\n(declined: stays 1350)\n    create entity \"order : Order\" as order order 100\n    waiter --> order : <<create>> Order(item, 1080, 2)\n    note right of order : billCents set once in the\\nconstructor, no setter:\\nprice locked at 1080\n    waiter -> vip : assignOrder(order)\n    note right of vip : SEATED to ORDERED\n  end\n  game -> cli : chefChoice = readChefChoice()\n  cli -> M : Chef: choose an order or WAIT?\n  M --> cli : the VIP's order\n  opt chefChoice is an order\n    game -> chef : prepare(order)\n    chef -> order : advancePreparation()\n    note right of order : Burger 1/1 unit done: PLACED to READY.\\nThe Waiter already acted this turn,\\nso it cannot be served before turn 3\n  end\n\n  game -> game : **5 Waiting and abandonment** processEndOfTurnUpdates()\n  loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()\n    game -> vip : reduceSatisfaction()\n    note right of vip : ORDERED, not yet served: 100 to 95\\n(abandonment check as in turn 1)\n  end\n  game -> hh : endTurn()\n  note right of hh : remainingTurns 1 to 0\\nHappy Hour is over\n\n  == Turn 3: Waiter serves the dish that became READY on an earlier turn ==\n\n  game -> game : **2-3 Manager choices and staff actions** coordinateStaffActions()\n  loop until the Waiter choice is accepted\n    game -> cli : choice = readWaiterChoice()\n    cli -> M : Waiter: SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT?\n    M --> cli : SERVE\n    opt choice == SERVE\n      game -> waiter : serveOrder(vip, 3)\n      waiter -> order : ready = isReady()\n      alt ready (finished on turn 2)\n        waiter -> order : markServed()\n        note right of order : READY to SERVED\n        waiter -> vip : markServed(3)\n        note right of vip : ORDERED to SERVED, servedTurn = 3\n        waiter -> order : turnTaken = getTurnTaken()\n        waiter -> vip : onServed(3 - turnTaken)\n        note right of vip : polymorphic hook: Critic loses 10 if served\\nmore than 2 turns after ordering;\\nVIP does nothing\n      else not READY (for example, SERVE tried on turn 2)\n        waiter --> game : rejects: \"order is not READY yet\"\n        game -> cli : displayError(reason)\n        note right of cli : staff task is not used up,\\nthe manager chooses again\n      end\n    end\n  end\n  note over game, cli : Chef: manager picks WAIT\n  note over game : **5** served customers lose no satisfaction,\\nso the waiting loop has nothing to do\n\n  == Turn 4: the customer has eaten for one full turn and becomes READY_TO_PAY ==\n\n  note over game, cli : Manager leaves Waiter and Chef at WAIT\n  game -> game : **4 Eating and payment** coordinatePaymentProcessing()\n  game -> rest : customers = getCustomers()\n  opt vip is SERVED and 4 > vip.getServedTurn() (3)\n    game -> vip : markReadyToPay(4)\n    note right of vip : SERVED to READY_TO_PAY, readyTurn = 4\n  end\n  note over game, cashier : Cashier does not charge yet:\\n4 > vip.getReadyTurn() (4) is false\n\n  == Turn 5: Cashier collects the locked bill ==\n\n  note over game, cli : Manager leaves Waiter and Chef at WAIT\n  game -> game : **4 Eating and payment** coordinatePaymentProcessing()\n  game -> rest : customers = getCustomers()\n  opt oldest READY_TO_PAY customer is vip and 5 > vip.getReadyTurn() (4)\n    game -> cashier : processPayment(vip, restaurant)\n    cashier -> vip : order = getOrder()\n    cashier -> order : bill = getBillCents()\n    note right of order : 1080: the locked price,\\nnot recalculated\n    cashier -> rest : recordPayment(bill)\n    note right of rest : revenue += $10.80\\nvip counts as paid with satisfaction 95\n    cashier -> vip : markPaid()\n    note right of vip : READY_TO_PAY to PAID\n    cashier -> order : markPaid()\n    note right of order : SERVED to PAID\n    cashier -> rest : releaseTableOf(vip)\n    rest -> t1 : release()\n  end\n\n  ... turns 6 to 18 run the same six steps for the other customers ...\nend\n\ngame -> game : **6 Result** evaluateGameResult()\ngame -> rest : paid = getPaidCustomerCount()\ngame -> rest : revenue = getRevenueCents()\ngame -> rest : avg = getAverageSatisfaction()\nnote right of rest : won = paid >= 4 and revenue >= 4500\\nand avg >= 60 (the game never ends early)\ngame -> cli : displayResult(won, restaurant)\nalt won\n  cli -> M : Victory\nelse not won\n  cli -> M : Defeat\nend\n@enduml\n"
}

### Tool result: toolu_01DuWzVsc8mTPr6jMiESmPca

1	// Re-renders every .puml in this folder to a one-page vector .pdf.
2	//
3	//   node docs/diagrams/render.mjs path/to/plantuml.jar
4	//
5	// Needs Java and a Chromium-based browser (set CHROME to override the lookup).
6	// The PDF page is sized to the diagram, so it has no margins. The intermediate
7	// SVG goes to a temp folder and is not kept.
8	import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
9	import fs from 'node:fs';
10	import os from 'node:os';
11	import path from 'node:path';
12	import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
13	
14	const dir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
15	const jar = process.argv[2] || process.env.PLANTUML_JAR;
16	if (!jar) {
17	  console.error('usage: node render.mjs path/to/plantuml.jar');
18	  process.exit(1);
19	}
20	
21	const chromeCandidates = [
22	  process.env.CHROME,
23	  'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
24	  'C:/Program Files (x86)/Microsoft/Edge/Application/msedge.exe',
25	  '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome',
26	  '/usr/bin/google-chrome',
27	  '/usr/bin/chromium',
28	].filter(Boolean);
29	const chrome = chromeCandidates.find((p) => fs.existsSync(p));
30	if (!chrome) {
31	  console.error('No Chrome/Edge found; set the CHROME environment variable.');
32	  process.exit(1);
33	}
34	
35	const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'rr-diagrams-'));
36	const sources = fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.puml'));
37	
38	for (const file of sources) {
39	  const name = file.replace(/\.puml$/, '');
40	  const svgPath = path.join(tmp, `${name}.svg`);
41	  execFileSync('java', ['-jar', jar, '-tsvg', '-charset', 'UTF-8', '-o', tmp, path.join(dir, file)], { stdio: 'inherit' });
42	
43	  const svg = fs.readFileSync(svgPath, 'utf8');
44	  const [, , w, h] = svg.match(/viewBox="([\d.\s]+)"/)[1].trim().split(/\s+/).map(Number);
45	  const width = Math.ceil(w);
46	  const height = Math.ceil(h);
47	
48	  const html = path.join(tmp, `${name}.html`);
49	  fs.writeFileSync(
50	    html,
51	    `<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><style>@page{size:${width}px ${height}px;margin:0}` +
52	      `html,body{margin:0;padding:0;width:${width}px;height:${height}px;overflow:hidden}svg{display:block}</style>` +
53	      svg.replace(/^<\?xml[^>]*>/, ''),
54	  );
55	
56	  const common = ['--headless=new', '--disable-gpu', '--hide-scrollbars'];
57	  execFileSync(chrome, [...common, '--no-pdf-header-footer', `--print-to-pdf=${path.join(dir, `${name}.pdf`)}`, pathToFileURL(html).href], { stdio: 'ignore' });
58	  console.log(`${name}: ${width}x${height}px -> ${name}.pdf`);
59	}
60	fs.rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true });
61

## 0260 User — 2026-11-02T15:15:16.067Z

Source record: 671; UUID: fce431af-dcc7-4634-9022-91704ed88f8a.

### Tool result: toolu_01CdGdAPrdGyz9mPYX2WcPcn

1	# Diagrams
2	
3	The `.puml` files are the sources of truth. The `.pdf` next to each one is generated (vector, one page); do not edit it by hand.
4	
5	| Diagram | Source | Notes |
6	|---|---|---|
7	| Stage 1 class | `stage-1-class.png` | Exported from Visual Paradigm. The source file is not in the repo yet. |
8	| Stage 2 class | `stage-2-class.puml` | Colour key: green new in Stage 2, yellow changed since Stage 1, blue unchanged. |
9	| Stage 3 class | `stage-3-class.puml` | Colour key: green new in Stage 3, yellow changed since Stage 2, blue unchanged. |
10	| Stage 3 sequence | `stage-3-sequence.puml` | A VIP orders a Burger with Happy Hour on. Turns 1 to 5 are drawn one by one. |
11	
12	Plain accessors that no other diagram uses are left out of the class diagrams. Operations that the sequence diagram calls are always listed.
13	
14	## Regenerating
15	
16	You need Java, a Chromium-based browser and a PlantUML jar (1.2025.4 was used):
17	
18	```
19	node docs/diagrams/render.mjs path/to/plantuml.jar
20	```
21	
22	This renders every `.puml` in the folder to a one-page vector `.pdf` sized to the diagram. Set `CHROME` if the script cannot find your browser. If you need a PNG or SVG for a document, export it from the PDF or run PlantUML directly; those formats are not kept in the repo.
23	
24	The class diagrams use PlantUML's built-in Smetana layout, so Graphviz is not needed. Smetana cannot route labelled same-row links, so sideways links carry role names at their ends instead of a mid-link label.
25	
26	## Keeping diagrams in step with the code
27	
28	Each diagram must match the code of the stage it describes. Before tagging a stage, check the class diagram's operations and the sequence diagram's messages against that tag, then re-render.
29

~~~json
{
  "file_path": "d:\\Java Projects\\SC2002GroupProject\\docs\\diagrams\\src\\render.mjs",
  "content": "// Re-renders every .puml in this folder to a vector .pdf in the parent folder.\n//\n//   node docs/diagrams/src/render.mjs path/to/plantuml.jar\n//\n// Needs Java and a Chromium-based browser (set CHROME to override the lookup).\n// A diagram that PlantUML splits with `newpage` becomes a multi-page PDF. Every\n// page is sized to the largest diagram page, so there are no margins. The\n// intermediate SVG files go to a temp folder and are not kept.\nimport { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';\nimport fs from 'node:fs';\nimport os from 'node:os';\nimport path from 'node:path';\nimport { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';\n\nconst dir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\nconst outDir = path.dirname(dir);\nconst jar = process.argv[2] || process.env.PLANTUML_JAR;\nif (!jar) {\n  console.error('usage: node render.mjs path/to/plantuml.jar');\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n\nconst chromeCandidates = [\n  process.env.CHROME,\n  'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',\n  'C:/Program Files (x86)/Microsoft/Edge/Application/msedge.exe',\n  '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome',\n  '/usr/bin/google-chrome',\n  '/usr/bin/chromium',\n].filter(Boolean);\nconst chrome = chromeCandidates.find((p) => fs.existsSync(p));\nif (!chrome) {\n  console.error('No Chrome/Edge found; set the CHROME environment variable.');\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n\nconst tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'rr-diagrams-'));\nconst sources = fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.puml'));\n\nfor (const file of sources) {\n  const name = file.replace(/\\.puml$/, '');\n  execFileSync('java', ['-jar', jar, '-tsvg', '-charset', 'UTF-8', '-o', tmp, path.join(dir, file)], { stdio: 'inherit' });\n\n  // PlantUML writes name.svg, then name_001.svg, name_002.svg, ... for each newpage.\n  const pageFiles = fs\n    .readdirSync(tmp)\n    .filter((f) => new RegExp(`^${name}(_\\\\d{3})?\\\\.svg$`).test(f))\n    .sort();\n  const pages = pageFiles.map((f) => {\n    const svg = fs.readFileSync(path.join(tmp, f), 'utf8');\n    const [, , w, h] = svg.match(/viewBox=\"([\\d.\\s]+)\"/)[1].trim().split(/\\s+/).map(Number);\n    return { svg: svg.replace(/^<\\?xml[^>]*>/, ''), width: Math.ceil(w), height: Math.ceil(h) };\n  });\n  const width = Math.max(...pages.map((p) => p.width));\n  const height = Math.max(...pages.map((p) => p.height));\n\n  const html = path.join(tmp, `${name}.html`);\n  fs.writeFileSync(\n    html,\n    `<!doctype html><meta charset=\"utf-8\"><style>@page{size:${width}px ${height}px;margin:0}` +\n      `html,body{margin:0;padding:0}svg{display:block}` +\n      `.page{width:${width}px;height:${height}px;overflow:hidden;break-after:page}.page:last-child{break-after:auto}</style>` +\n      pages.map((p) => `<div class=\"page\">${p.svg}</div>`).join(''),\n  );\n\n  const common = ['--headless=new', '--disable-gpu', '--hide-scrollbars'];\n  execFileSync(chrome, [...common, '--no-pdf-header-footer', `--print-to-pdf=${path.join(outDir, `${name}.pdf`)}`, pathToFileURL(html).href], { stdio: 'ignore' });\n  console.log(`${name}: ${pages.length} page(s) of ${width}x${height}px -> ${name}.pdf`);\n}\nfs.rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true });\n"
}
~~~

  const base = 'http://127.0.0.1:48213/view.html?f=';
  const out = 'd:\\Java Projects\\SC2002GroupProject\\.playwright-mcp\\';
  const jobs = [
    ['stage-3-sequence.pdf', 1, 2306, [0, 1080], 2145, 'q1'],
    ['stage-3-sequence.pdf', 2, 2306, [0, 1080], 1847, 'q2'],
    ['stage-3-sequence.pdf', 3, 2306, [0], 1257, 'q3'],
  ];
  const made = [];
  for (const [f, p, w, ys, h, n] of jobs) {
    await page.setViewportSize({ width: w, height: 1100 });
    await page.goto(base + f + '&p=' + p + '&s=1&x=0&y=0');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.title === 'done', null, { timeout: 30000 });
    for (let i = 0; i < ys.length; i++) {
      await page.evaluate(y => { document.getElementById('c').style.top = (-y) + 'px'; }, ys[i]);
      await page.screenshot({ path: out + n + '_' + i + '.png' });
      made.push(n + '_' + i + '.png');
    }
  }
  return made;
})(page);
```

~~~json
{
  "file_path": "d:\\Java Projects\\SC2002GroupProject\\docs\\diagrams\\src\\README.md",
  "content": "# Diagrams\n\nThe `.puml` files in this folder (`docs/diagrams/src/`) are the sources of truth. The `.pdf` files one level up (`docs/diagrams/`) are generated (vector); do not edit them by hand.\n\n| Diagram | Source | PDF | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Stage 1 class | `stage-1-class.puml` | `stage-1-class.pdf` | Redrawn in PlantUML from the Visual Paradigm model `stage-1-class.uml` (kept as the archive). Every class is new. |\n| Stage 2 class | `stage-2-class.puml` | `stage-2-class.pdf` | Colour key: green new in Stage 2, yellow changed since Stage 1, blue unchanged. |\n| Stage 3 class | `stage-3-class.puml` | `stage-3-class.pdf` | Colour key: green new in Stage 3, yellow changed since Stage 2, blue unchanged. |\n| Stage 3 sequence | `stage-3-sequence.puml` | `stage-3-sequence.pdf` | A VIP orders a Burger with Happy Hour on. Turns 1 to 5 are drawn one by one over three pages (setup and turn 1; turns 2 and 3; turns 4 and 5 and the result). |\n\nPlain accessors that no other diagram uses are left out of the class diagrams. Operations that the sequence diagram calls are always listed, and every message in the sequence diagram is an operation declared in the Stage 3 class diagram.\n\n## Regenerating\n\nYou need Java, a Chromium-based browser and a PlantUML jar (1.2025.4 was used):\n\n```\nnode docs/diagrams/src/render.mjs path/to/plantuml.jar\n```\n\nThis renders every `.puml` in this folder to a vector `.pdf` in `docs/diagrams/`. A diagram that PlantUML splits with `newpage` becomes a multi-page PDF, with every page sized to the largest page. Set `CHROME` if the script cannot find your browser. If you need a PNG or SVG for a document, export it from the PDF or run PlantUML directly; those formats are not kept in the repo.\n\nThe class diagrams use PlantUML's built-in Smetana layout, so Graphviz is not needed. Smetana cannot route labelled same-row links, so sideways links carry no mid-link label.\n\n## Keeping diagrams in step with the code\n\nEach diagram must match the code of the stage it describes. Before tagging a stage, check the class diagram's operations and the sequence diagram's messages against that tag, then re-render.\n\nCheck these against the code first, because the diagrams were updated without access to it:\n\n- Stage 1 to 2: `Customer.canFinishEating(turn)`, `Customer.canPay(turn)` and `Restaurant.findOldestEligiblePayer(turn)` appear in Stage 1 but not in Stages 2 and 3. The Stage 2 key lists them as removed, with the turn guards now checked in `GameController`.\n- `Restaurant.getCustomers()` and `Restaurant.getWaitingQueue()` are listed in all three class diagrams because the sequence diagram calls `getCustomers()`.\n- In the sequence diagram, `Waiter.serveOrder` reports an invalid serve by rejecting the call (drawn as a dashed return to `game`), and the `startGame()` setup calls come from the earlier code-derived sequence diagram.\n- The scenario assumes the first arrival (turn 1) is a VIP who picks the Burger under `customerId % menu.size()`.\n"
}
~~~
